**Protocol limits (preparation only):** Use aware UTC half-open `[start,end)` windows, retain the saved timezone and timestamp provenance, and account for DST's 23/24/25-hour local days. Exclude missing, stale, invalid or unknown intervals from point estimates and disclose excluded duration/reasons, never zero-fill or infer completeness from a 14-day span. Flag overrides, boosts and context changes; absent intervention records do not prove no interventions. Current APIs omit some raw counter, sensor and native-policy provenance; source availability does not establish populated, fresh or complete data. Accepted future P2.2 elapsed-time scoring holds a trusted sample forward for at most 15 minutes, ending at the next reading and splitting at target boundaries, with no backward fill/interpolation across gaps; unknown duration cannot improve the score. Current `comfort_outcome()` is sample-count based: no new reporting is implemented or authorized.

## How this document is maintained

- This is the single user-facing status for the ML-optimization roadmap.
- Implementer and Test Engineer update their item's status, evidence, and one activity line before returning.
- Read-only specialists return a TRACKER line. Delivery Lead forwards it to an editing specialist and ensures every gate result is recorded.
- Delivery Lead records your decisions and approvals, and arranges a final tracker-only sync after the final gate.
- Update “At a glance” when the phase, next step, or waiting-on-you changes. Keep the update date and role current.
- Mark 🟢 Done only after all required gates pass, including Release Gate PASS.
- Use short, plain-English notes. Do not copy long logs or secrets here.
- Log paths point to the git-ignored `.agent-runs` folder. They are local evidence, not published documentation.
